*Souranil Kahali,Rituparna Bose,Abner Hernandez,Tomas Arias-Vergara,Andreas Maier,Ning Ma,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过逐层编码器分析，探究了多语言医疗适应如何重塑Whisper模型的内部表征。研究发现微调显著提升了MedASR性能，且最佳模型取决于具体适配设置；同时揭示了英语医疗微调是主要的表征变化驱动力，而多语言续训练则有助于保留已适配的表征空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Whisper等大型预训练ASR模型具有强大的泛化能力，但其在经过医疗和多语言适应后的行为特征，除了词错误率（WER）之外，其内部机制尚不明确。

Method: 对Whisper不同规模模型进行逐层编码器分析，比较了零样本解码、仅英语微调、仅德语诊断微调、两阶段EN->EN+DE延续训练以及直接EN+DE联合微调等多种策略。

Result: 微调显著改善了MedASR性能，但最佳模型因适配场景而异：Whisper-Medium在直接EN+DE训练中取得最低综合WER (26.30%) 和最低英语WER (7.72%)；Whisper-Large-v3在德语测试中表现最好 (44.96%)，但该结果基于小样本单说话人数据，泛化性存疑。

Conclusion: 英语医疗微调导致了主导性的编码器表征变化，而多语言续训练主要起到保留已适配表征空间的作用。随着WER降低，线性可恢复的错误预测线索减弱，但领域和语言信息在各层中仍高度可恢复。

Abstract: Medical automatic speech recognition (MedASR) requires adaptation to specialised terminology, limited annotated clinical data, and multilingual use cases. Although large-scale pretrained ASR models such as Whisper achieve strong generalisation, their behaviour after medical and multilingual adaptation remains insufficiently understood beyond word error rate (WER). This paper investigates how multilingual medical adaptation reshapes the internal representations of Whisper models through layer-wise encoder analysis. We compare zero-shot decoding, English-only fine-tuning, German-only diagnostic fine-tuning, two-stage EN->EN+DE continuation, and direct EN+DE fine-tuning across Whisper model sizes. Fine-tuning substantially improves MedASR performance, but the best model depends on the adaptation setting: Whisper-Medium gives the lowest English WER (7.72%) and the lowest combined EN+DE WER under direct EN+DE training (26.30%); German-only Whisper-Large-v3 gives the lowest German WER (44.96%), but as a within-corpus diagnostic on 86 single-speaker training utterances rather than robust generalisation. Layer-wise analysis of the two-stage Whisper-Small trajectory shows that English medical fine-tuning produces the dominant encoder shift, whereas multilingual continuation largely preserves the adapted representation space. Domain and language information remain highly recoverable across layers, while linearly recoverable error-predictive cues weaken as WER improves.

</details>


### [143] [Assessing Quality of Experience in Natural Language Generation of German Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18888)
*Dinh Nam Pham,Shushen Manakhimova,Vivien Macketanz,Sebastian Möller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TextQ-German数据集，用于从体验质量（QoE）角度评估德语自然语言生成（NLG）。通过众包研究收集人类评分并识别感知维度，开发了自动QoE预测模型。混合模型表现最佳，数据集包含LLM生成的输出，验证了泛化能力，为NLG评估提供了公共资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型等NLG系统在现实世界中的广泛部署，可靠的文本评估变得至关重要。然而，传统的自动指标无法捕捉感知质量的多面性，因此需要一种以人为中心、基于体验质量（QoE）的评估方法。

Method: 1. 构建TextQ-German数据集，涵盖自动文本摘要和机器翻译任务。
2. 通过德语用户的众包研究收集人类质量评分，识别相关感知质量维度。
3. 开发三种自动QoE预测模型：基于Transformer的模型、基于语言学特征的模型以及混合模型。
4. 扩展数据集，加入由LLM生成并标注了总体QoE分数的输出。
5. 在保留集上进行最终验证，测试模型的泛化能力。

Result: 1. 混合模型在几乎所有实验设置中均优于纯Transformer基线模型。
2. 仅使用语言学特征的方法也能接近微调语言模型的性能。
3. 最终验证表明模型在未见过数据上具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一个公开可用的NLG评估资源及自动QoE预测基线，有助于开发更符合人类质量感知的NLG系统。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Natural Language Generation (NLG) has made the reliable evaluation of generated text increasingly critical, as these systems, such as large language models (LLMs), are now widely deployed in real-world applications. However, traditional automatic metrics fail to capture the multifaceted nature of perceived quality. In this paper, we introduce TextQ-German, a novel dataset suite for human-centered evaluation of German NLG from a Quality of Experience (QoE) perspective, covering automatic text summarization and machine translation. Through crowdsourcing studies with German speakers, we collect human quality ratings and identify relevant perceptual quality dimensions for each task. We develop automatic QoE prediction models, including transformer-based, linguistic feature-based, and hybrid approaches. Hybrid models outperform pure transformer baselines in almost all experimental settings, while linguistic features alone can approach the performance of fine-tuned language models. The dataset is extended with LLM-generated outputs annotated with overall QoE scores. Final validation on held-out sets indicates generalization to unseen data. Our work contributes a publicly accessible resource for NLG evaluation and baselines for automatic QoE prediction, providing a foundation for developing NLG systems that better align with human quality perception.

</details>


### [144] [SMTrap: Cost-Effective DoS Attacks Against Large Reasoning Models via SMT Conflict Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18921)
*Jian Yang,Zhenqi Feng,Zhaoyang Yu,Zhaoxin Fan,Kejian Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Jianjun Huang,Wei You,Bin Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SMTrap，一种无需模型反馈、仅用CPU的低成本LRM拒绝服务攻击方法。利用SMT冲突计数作为信号引导生成推理密集型CSP实例，显著增加大语言模型的回溯搜索时间和输出长度，实现比现有基线强得多的DoS效果，并提供缓解工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型拒绝服务（LRM-DoS）方法严重依赖模型反馈，需要重复查询目标模型或训练专用攻击模型，这些操作成本高且削弱了攻击效率。

Method: 提出“搜索放大”范式，使用可满足性模理论（SMT）求解器导出的冲突计数作为低成本外部信号。观察到LRM在解决约束满足问题（CSP）时依赖试错和回溯搜索，SMT冲突计数与LRM的回溯深度及输出轨迹长度正相关。基于此构建轻量级CPU框架SMTrap，生成推理密集型CSP查询，无需模型查询、攻击模型训练或GPU计算。

Result: 在七个前沿模型上的评估显示，SMTrap实现了最先进的LRM-DoS能力，其产生的拒绝服务效果比现有基线强数倍。同时展示了基于工具的缓解方案，可显著减少token消耗。

Conclusion: SMTrap通过利用SMT冲突计数引导CSP实例生成，有效规避了传统LRM-DoS方法的高昂成本，证明了无需模型反馈的攻击策略的高效性，并为防御提供了新思路。

Abstract: Existing LRM-DoS methods rely heavily on model feedback to synthesize attack queries, requiring either repeated queries to the target model or training a dedicated attack model. These expensive operations severely weaken attack leverage. In this paper, we propose \emph{search amplification}, a novel, model-feedback-free LRM-DoS paradigm. It employs the conflict count derived from an Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) solver as a low-cost external signal to guide the synthesis of inference-heavy Constraint Satisfaction Problem (CSP) instances. Our key observation is that LRMs depend on trial-and-backtracking search when solving CSPs, where higher SMT conflict counts on a given CSP instance positively correlate with more extensive LRM backtracking search and substantially longer output trajectories. Building on this finding, we propose \textsc{SMTrap}, a lightweight, CPU-only framework. Guided by SMT conflict counts, \textsc{SMTrap} generates inference-heavy CSP queries without model queries, attack-model training, or GPU computation. Evaluations across seven frontier models demonstrate the state-of-the-art LRM-DoS capability of \textsc{SMTrap}, producing DoS effects multiple times stronger than existing baselines. To mitigate the threat of \textsc{SMTrap}, we demonstrate a tool-based mitigation that significantly cuts token usage.

</details>


### [145] [Test-Time Scaling in the Wild: Why Exploitation, Not Exploration, Is the Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18931)
*Davide Romano,Kanak Raj,Jerrod Parker,Daniele Giofrè*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示了测试时扩展（TTS）在开放域生成中的局限性：虽然增加计算量能提升候选集质量，但现有奖励模型与真实质量相关性极低（ρ≈0.12），导致从候选集中选择最终输出的“利用”步骤几乎随机失效。除融合方法外，其他TTS技术未能显著超越单样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时扩展（TTS）技术主要在验证简单的任务（如数学和代码）上得到开发，缺乏在需要复杂判断的开放域生成任务（如医学、法律、金融等）上的系统性评估。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过统一框架分析TTS在不同领域的有效性。

Method: 构建了统一的理论框架，将TTS方法的效能分解为“探索”（生成候选）和“利用”（选择最优）两个部分。对五种TTS家族在五个开放域基准（医学、法律、金融、通用聊天、创意写作）进行了计算归一化的比较实验。

Result: 1. 探索有效：随着计算资源增加，候选池中的最佳候选者质量稳步提升。
2. 利用失败：由于奖励模型与真实质量相关性极低（ρ_v ≈ 0.12），从候选池中选出最终输出的过程近乎随机；树搜索因多样性崩溃加剧了这一问题。
3. 方法表现：仅“融合”（Fusion）方法在所有基准上稳定优于单样本基线，但仍仅恢复约40%的可用质量；其他方法的增益存在混淆或仅在单一基准有效。
4. 核心瓶颈：主要瓶颈不在于生成候选集，而在于从中进行选择。

Conclusion: 当前的测试时扩展技术在开放域生成中面临严重瓶颈，主要源于现有奖励模型无法有效指导输出选择。未来改进应聚焦于提升选择机制的准确性，而非单纯增加候选数量。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves language model outputs by spending additional inference compute - generating multiple candidates, searching over partial sequences, or iteratively refining drafts. These techniques yield large gains on mathematics and code, but have been developed and stress-tested almost exclusively on tasks where verification is straightforward. We conduct the first compute-normalised comparison of five TTS families across five open-ended generation benchmarks spanning medicine, law, finance, general chat, and creative writing - grounded in a unified framework that decomposes the effectiveness of each method's token budget into exploration and exploitation. The answer depends on which side of that decomposition you examine. Scaling exploration works: the best candidate in the pool improves steadily with compute across all settings. What breaks is exploitation - the step that converts a rich candidate pool into a final output. With state-of-the-art generators, reward models correlate at only $ρ_v \approx 0.12$ with true quality, rendering selection near-random regardless of budget. Tree search amplifies this failure through diversity collapse. Refinement helps on one of five benchmarks; its apparent gains elsewhere are confounded. Only synthesis across candidates (Fusion) consistently improves over single-sample baselines, yet still recovers only ~40% of available quality. The candidate pool is not the bottleneck - choosing from it is.

</details>


### [146] [MedUAG: Unified Understanding and Generation for Medical Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18937)
*Zijie Meng,Yuncheng Zhang,Hualiang Wang,Yitian Tang,Xiaotang Gai,Chen Shen,Songtao Jiang,Shaosheng Cao,Jian Wu,Xian Wu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对医学领域缺乏统一理解与生成（UAG）基准和模型的痛点，构建了包含600万实例的MedUAGCorpus数据集、涵盖12项任务的MedUAGBench评估基准，并开发了端到端训练的MedUAG模型，显著提升了医学多模态任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型正发展为统一的理解与生成框架，但在医学领域的应用受到两个主要障碍的限制：一是缺乏全面的训练和评估基准，二是缺乏经过广泛验证的统一医学模型。

Method: 1. 构建MedUAGCorpus：这是迄今为止最大的统一医学理解与生成数据集，包含超过600万个实例，涵盖14种成像模态。
2. 引入MedUAGBench：一个系统性的基准测试，在标准化协议下将医学生成评估扩展到12种多样化的任务。
3. 开发MedUAG模型：利用上述资源，训练了一个端到端的统一医学模型。

Result: 广泛的实验表明，MedUAG在多种理解和生成任务中均取得了强劲的性能表现，确立了一个具有竞争力的基线。

Conclusion: 该研究为下一代医学多模态系统奠定了基础，推动了医学领域统一理解与生成框架的发展。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are rapidly evolving into unified understanding and generation (UAG) frameworks. However, extending these unified paradigms to the medical domain is hindered by: the absence of comprehensive training and evaluation benchmarks, and the lack of broadly validated unified medical model. To address these gaps, we present a comprehensive foundation for medical UAG. First, we construct MedUAGCorpus, the largest unified medical understanding and generation dataset to date, comprising over 6 million instances across 14 imaging modalities. Second, we introduce MedUAGBench, a systematic benchmark that expands medical generation evaluation to 12 diverse tasks under standardized protocols. Finally, leveraging these resources, we develop MedUAG, an end-to-end trained unified medical model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MedUAG achieves strong performance across a wide array of understanding and generation tasks, establishing a competitive baseline and paving the way for next-generation medical multimodal systems.

</details>


### [147] [Institutional Newspapers Pipeline: Deriving billions of high quality tokens from historical newspapers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18972)
*Matteo Cargnelutti,Catherine Brobston,Eben English,Jake Sadow,Kacie Bailey,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jessica Chapel,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出“机构报纸流水线”（Institutional Newspapers Pipeline），一种模块化、可解释且计算高效的系统，用于从历史报纸扫描中提取高质量结构化数据。该流水线在波士顿公共图书馆的馆藏上运行，释放了包含163亿token和8310万个图像块的大型开放数据集，旨在解锁数千万份历史报纸扫描的高价值数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史报纸记录了丰富的公共生活，但其密集、不规则且有时嘈杂的版面布局使得计算访问这些材料既具有挑战性又受到限制。现有的计算方法难以高效地从这些非结构化扫描中获取高质量的结构化数据。

Method: 设计了与波士顿公共图书馆联合开发的模块化流水线，确保每一步都可解释且可定制，并能在工作站级硬件上以计算节俭的方式运行。流程包括：将扫描分割为类型无关的图像块（crops）；对每个图像块进行光学字符识别（OCR）；随后执行文本分析、类型分类、阅读顺序检测、命名实体识别、主题分类、语言检测和预计算嵌入生成等步骤。

Result: 处理了1,473,635份1795年至1930年间出版的公共领域报纸扫描。生成的OCR输出涵盖163亿个o200k_base token，涉及8310万个独立图像块。发布了配套的流水线、训练的小模型以及大规模评估结果和测量数据作为开放资源。

Conclusion: 这项工作是通过解锁数千万份历史报纸扫描中的高质量数据所迈出的重要一步，为后续的历史文献数字化分析和研究提供了坚实的基础设施和数据集支持。

Abstract: Historical newspapers are an abundant record of public life, but their dense, irregular and sometimes noisy layouts make computational access to these materials both challenging and limited. We present the Institutional Newspapers Pipeline, a modular system we jointly designed with Boston Public Library to extract high-quality, structured datasets from historical newspaper scans. It was architected so that each step remains interpretable and customizable, and so that the pipeline as a whole remains computationally frugal enough to run on workstation-level hardware. The pipeline runs each scan through a multi-step process: it segments scans into individual type-agnostic crops and performs OCR on each resulting segment before then performing text analysis, type classification, reading order detection, named entities recognition, subject classification, language detection, and pre-computed embeddings generation on every crop. We ran this pipeline against a portion of Boston Public Library's holdings and released the results as an open dataset. The optical character recognition (OCR) output represents 16.3 billion o200k_base tokens across 83.1 million individual crops, extracted from 1,473,635 public domain newspaper scans published between 1795 and 1930. This report describes our methods for each processing step, the small models we trained, as well as the evaluation results and dataset-scale measurements we collected in the process. It accompanies the release of the pipeline, models, and dataset. We position this work as a substantial step towards unlocking high-quality data from tens of millions of newspaper scans.

</details>


### [148] [DeepWeaver: Bridging the Evidence Synthesis Gap in Open-Ended Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18988)
*Xujia Wang,Yizhe Zhang,Bin Xu,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DeepWeaver框架，通过维护结构化思维链（TBCs）解决检索与生成之间的证据合成鸿沟，显著提升开放域问答的内容充分性、引用准确性和细节保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的“检索-生成”流水线中，仅靠检索不足以应对噪声和碎片化信息，直接生成常导致证据利用不足、引用错位及信息浅层化，存在显著的“证据合成鸿沟”。

Method: 提出DeepWeaver框架，采用思维块链（TBCs）作为结构化表示来组织主张、关键信息和证据；利用子级TBCs检查剩余证据、修订内容并发现新主张，最终生成综合答案。同时引入LoQA基准进行评估。

Result: 在LoQA和DeepResearch Bench上评估显示，DeepWever在多种LLM上提升了内容充分性、引用接地性和细节保留率，实现了更深入的洞察力和更高的引用质量。

Conclusion: 证据编织（evidence weaving）是弥合开放域问答中检索与生成之间差距的有效机制。

Abstract: Retrieve-then-generate pipelines are commonly used to produce deep-research answers for open-ended questions, but retrieval alone is insufficient: LLMs must organize noisy and fragmented evidence into comprehensive, well-cited answers. We refer to this process as evidence synthesis. However, direct generation often underuses evidence, misaligns citations, and collapses diverse information into shallow summaries, exposing an evidence synthesis gap between retrieval and generation. Thus, we propose DeepWeaver, a novel framework that weaves noisy retrieved evidence into comprehensive answers by maintaining Thought Block Chains (TBCs), a structured representation that groups claims, salient information, keywords, and supporting evidence. DeepWeaver uses subordinate TBCs to inspect residual evidence, commit TBC revisions, and discover new claims before final generation. We evaluate DeepWeaver on open-ended QA over both knowledge bases and the web, and introduce LoQA, a high-density benchmark for evidence synthesis. Across multiple LLMs, DeepWeaver improves content sufficiency, citation grounding, and detail preservation on LoQA, while achieving deeper insights and higher citation quality on DeepResearch Bench. These results show that evidence weaving is an effective mechanism for bridging retrieval and generation in open-ended QA. Our code is available at https://github.com/KlozeWang/DeepWeaver.

</details>


### [149] [Structure, Association, and Decision Value: Representation-Based Difficulty Estimation for Adaptive Inference in African-Language NLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19003)
*Toheeb Ogunade*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究证明在非洲多语言NLP中，内部表示统计量无法提供有效的示例级难度信号以支持自适应推理。由于数据泄露、模型能力与参数量无显著关联、以及统计指标对计算收益的预测存在片面性，自适应路由并未优于始终使用昂贵推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究内部表示统计特征（如角离散度、有效秩）能否作为有用的示例级难度信号，用于在多语言非洲NLP场景中进行自适应推理优化。

Method: 在15种非洲语言上基于冻结的现成检查点研究自然语言推理（NLI）。分析了AfriXNLI数据集与XNLI的数据重叠情况，比较了不同参数规模模型的跨语言能力，评估了三种多语言表示空间中的统计特性，并检验了这些统计量与推理成本及准确率提升之间的关联性。

Result: 1. AfriXNLI英文配置与XNLI测试集高度重合，导致其无法作为干净的评价基准；2. 参数规模不能可靠地决定模型在非洲语言上的能力优劣，大模型并非在所有语言上都更好；3. 角离散度受语言影响大于有效秩，池化相关系数会掩盖真实关系；4. 不同的统计信号仅能预测特定的计算收益（如概率增益 vs 预测改变），且两者相关性低（0.655）；5. 在所测条件下，无任何信号使自适应路由优于始终进行昂贵推理，尽管Oracle方案在60%算力下可提升11个准确率点。

Conclusion: 单一的内部表示统计量可能在一个计算收益概念上显著，而在另一个概念上无关，因此不适合作为决策变量。自适应推理在此类设置中缺乏实际优势。

Abstract: We ask whether internal representation statistics can provide useful example-level difficulty signals for adaptive inference in multilingual African NLP, and find that they cannot in this setting. Studying natural language inference across 15 African languages with frozen off-the-shelf checkpoints, we report four results. First, AfriXNLI's English configuration shares 1,047 of its 1,050 examples verbatim with XNLI evaluation data, and one widely used NLI checkpoint scores 1.000 on that test split, consistent with XNLI test exposure. Because AfriXNLI is derived from XNLI, its English, French and Swahili configurations cannot serve as clean evaluations for XNLI-trained models. Second, parameter count does not reliably order capability across African languages: our larger checkpoint is better in seven languages and worse in eight, with no significant aggregate difference. Third, across three multilingual representation spaces, angular dispersion is consistently more language-determined than effective rank, so pooled correlations can inflate one and mask the other. Fourth, the association that survives language control depends on the target: effective rank predicts probability gain from escalation but not whether escalation changes the prediction, while cheap-model confidence shows the opposite pattern; the two targets correlate at only 0.655. Under the tested models, signals, and compute budgets, no evaluated signal makes adaptive routing preferable to always-expensive inference, although an oracle exceeds it by 11 accuracy points at 60% of the compute. Our central methodological finding is that a representation statistic can be statistically significant for one notion of computational benefit while being irrelevant to another, and therefore be a poor decision variable.

</details>


### [150] [Introducing the Privacy-HSD Trade-off: Hate Speech Detection, but not at the Cost of Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19006)
*Stephen Meisenbacher,Vlad Garbuz,Chirill Donos,Maxim Dnestreanschii,Gabriel Creanga,Andreea-Elena Bodea,Thomas Lampert,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自动仇恨言论检测（HSD）系统在保护用户隐私方面存在的隐患，即模型可能通过编码作者身份特征来换取性能提升。为此，研究提出了“隐私-HSD权衡”概念，并通过基准测试及新提出的领域特定技术AgnoSpeech，证明了在平衡隐私保护与检测性能方面的可行性与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论对在线用户（尤其是青年和少数群体构成严重威胁，构建可靠、鲁棒的自动仇恨言论检测系统至关重要。然而，现有的HSD系统往往忽视了个人隐私权，存在无意中泄露作者身份信息以优化检测性能的风险，亟需在检测效能与隐私保护之间寻求平衡。

Method: 研究首先分析了HSD系统与隐私保护的交集，指出了模型可能因编码作者身份而威胁隐私的问题。随后，建立了“隐私-HSD权衡”的理论框架。在此基础上，对一系列文本去隐私化方法进行了基准测试，并引入了一种新提出的领域特定技术——AgnoSpeech，以评估其在平衡隐私与检测性能方面的表现。

Result: 研究表明，在保持仇恨言论检测性能的同时实现有效的隐私保护是困难的，但是可行的。AgnoSpeech及其他去隐私化方法的测试结果验证了这种权衡关系的存在，并展示了不同方法在处理这一矛盾时的效果差异。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在开发仇恨言论检测系统时，必须谨慎平衡隐私保护与检测效能之间的关系。这一发现为保护在线参与安全提供了重要依据，并呼吁未来开展更多关于隐私与HSD之间权衡机制的深入研究。

Abstract: Hate speech is a real and timely threat that affects a large portion of online users, especially youth and minority groups. While building reliable and robust automatic hate speech detection (HSD) systems is paramount, we argue that this must also be balanced with the individual right to privacy. Exploring the intersection of HSD and privacy, we demonstrate that HSD systems might unintentionally achieve performance at the cost of encoding authorship, posing a threat to privacy. Building on these findings, we establish the notion of a privacy-HSD trade-off, which demands a careful balance. We benchmark a series of text privatization methods, as well as our newly proposed domain-specific AgnoSpeech technique, showing that balancing privacy and HSD is difficult but feasible. The findings make a strong case for more research on the trade-offs between privacy and HSD, both of which have tangible implications for the safeguarding of online participation.

</details>


### [151] [Grading the Graders: Verification Autonomy Levels (L0-L5) for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19009)
*Yajie Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“验证自治层级”（VAL）标准，旨在澄清大语言模型（LLM）验证文献中术语混淆的问题。VAL根据验证规范的来源和结果保证性将验证方案分为L0至L5级，揭示了“完整性盲点”，并论证了形式化属性与经验开放世界验证的根本差异，解决了现有研究中概念混用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM验证文献中，“层级”一词含义模糊，至少包含五种不同定义（如验证粒度、概念抽象、风险等级等），导致系统性概念混淆，缺乏统一的分类标准来评估验证方案的可靠性和保证程度。

Method: 提出“验证自治层级”（VAL）元标准，沿单一维度对验证方案进行分类：基于验证规范的来源及判决的保证性。该分类涵盖从L0（LLM自我声明，无确定性锚点）到L3/L4（可判定系统，具有单属性或领域级完整性），并指出L5在非受限情况下不可能实现。通过四个领域（符号数学、行为监控、医疗诊断、代码生成）及最强形式的形式化验证基线进行文档记录和实证分析，对比其他被证明与VAL正交的概念（粒度、概念层次、风险、系统堆栈）。

Result: 1. 确立了VAL分类体系，明确了各级别的定义和局限性。2. 识别出“完整性盲点”：基于替换和采样的验证器只能确认候选方案成立，无法证明未遗漏任何候选方案。3. 揭示了一个未被明确指出的二分法：完整性仅可达于形式化可规范化的属性，而经验性开放世界验证（如事实核查、诊断）最高仅能达到有锚点的正确性（L2）。4. 证明了现有的粒度、概念层次、风险和系统堆栈概念与VAL正交，从而厘清了17篇调查论文中的概念混淆。

Conclusion: VAL为LLM验证提供了一个清晰、统一的分类框架，解决了文献中长期存在的术语混乱问题。研究强调，在追求更高程度的验证保证时，必须区分形式化属性的完整性与经验性验证的正确性，这对设计更可靠的LLM验证系统具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly paired with verifiers (step checkers, self-consistency filters, tool-based fact checkers, formal proof assistants) that claim to detect the model's errors. Yet the verification literature uses the word "level" to mean at least five different things: verification granularity, concept abstraction, risk tier, system-stack layer, and the epistemic source of the ground truth. We propose Verification Autonomy Levels (VAL), a meta-standard classifying verification schemes along a single axis: where does the verification spec come from, and what does the verdict guarantee? VAL ranges from L0 (LLM self-declaration, no deterministic anchor) through L2 (objective ground truth, correctness only) to L3/L4 (decidable systems with single-property or domain-level completeness), with L5 impossible in the unrestricted case. Central to VAL is the completeness blind spot: substitution- and sampling-based verifiers can confirm that proposed candidates hold, but cannot prove that no candidate was missed. We further identify a dichotomy the literature has not stated: completeness is reachable only for formally specifiable properties, while empirical open-world verification (fact-checking, diagnosis) caps at anchored correctness (L2). We document this across four domains (symbolic mathematics, behavior monitoring, medical diagnosis, and code generation) and in the strongest existing formal-verification baseline, whose authors note the verifier "focuses on the correctness of each step." We show the levels of granularity, concept hierarchy, risk, and system stack are orthogonal to VAL, resolving a systematic conflation across 17 surveyed papers. Code and full assessment are released as supplementary material.

</details>


### [152] [Institutional Books - Enriched Text: A customizable multilingual open-source pipeline for denoising, deduplicating, and annotating OCR text at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19026)
*David Lowry-Duda,Matteo Cargnelutti,Catherine Brobston,Salwa Ismail,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对哈佛图书馆数字藏书IB-HL，提出“Enriched Text”方法，通过保留元数据标注而非激进清洗，解决大规模预处理与精细信息保管间的矛盾，提供可定制的文本处理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模预处理流程倾向于针对网络文本进行激进过滤、去重和语言限制，导致有意义元数据丢失；同时研究人员重复劳动，缺乏兼顾信息保管与机器可读性的标准化方案。

Method: 提出“Enriched Text”方法，将文本规范化并保留元数据作为HTML-like层叠标注。具体包括分离附录、检测段落级语言、识别重复段落聚类及计算每段落比特/字节得分，支持多语言处理。

Result: 发布IB-HL-ET数据集，包含217B tokens（983,003卷，1.39B个带标注的子主题段落）及配套生成管道，实现了从单一令牌流到可解析、可定制信息的转变。

Conclusion: 该方案通过结构化标注使集合更易被机器解析和人工研究，赋予用户自定义处理输出的能力，平衡了数据处理效率与信息完整性。

Abstract: Released in 2025, Institutional Books: Harvard Library (IB-HL) is a collection of 983,004 volumes (242B o200k_base tokens), originally digitized through Harvard Library's participation in the Google Books Library project. As researchers and developers have begun to use IB-HL, a tension has emerged between standard large-scale preprocessing practices and the goals of careful information stewardship. Many existing pipelines optimize for web text: as a result, they tend to aggressively filter, deduplicate, restrict by language, and sometimes discard meaningful metadata. Meanwhile, researchers seeking to use IB-HL duplicate effort while performing similar processing and analysis.
  We describe an approach that we call Enriched Text. Instead of producing a single 'complete' stream of tokens, we normalize the text while preserving metadata through annotations. We separate endmatter, detect per-paragraph language, identify clusters of duplicate paragraphs, and compute per-paragraph bits-per-byte scores. We provide this information through HTML-like annotations layered on top of the text. By parsing these annotations, users can tailor the output to their own needs instead of accepting a global editorial decision on content. The pipeline applies to all $\approx$250 languages in the collection.
  This report describes this project's goals, implementation, and design rationale. The release includes IB-HL-ET (an enriched-text version of IB-HL containing 217B o200k_base tokens across 983,003 volumes, organized into 1.39B annotated subtopic paragraphs) and the pipeline that produced it. These serve to make the collection easier for machines to parse and for humans to study.

</details>


### [153] [Intercepting the Kangaroo: Experimental Astrolinguistics with Constructed Lexicons, Active Probing, and Large Language Models as Informants and Hypothesis Proposers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19124)
*Francesco Cordella,Mauro Cappelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究将天体语言学从纯理论推测转为实验验证，通过语言模型模拟不同分类体系间的翻译，提出了一套包含多种策略的协议，成功实现了零未检测误译，并证明了在噪声环境下协议的鲁棒性及对复杂词汇的有效恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Freudenthal（1960）以来，天体语言学（即与以不同方式对现实进行分类的意识进行交流）一直停留在纯推测阶段。旨在通过实验手段验证跨范畴系统间翻译的可行性及可靠性。

Method: 使用具有故意不兼容构词法的两个大型语言模型作为“信息员”（一个编码形状、颜色和运动；另一个融合颜色与运动，编码奇偶性且缺乏形状）。由完全脚本化的协调者在两者类别系统间进行翻译。测试协议包括：交叉情境消除、预注册预测探针、主动场景选择、更严格的恢复轮次以及隔离机制。此外，还引入了生成-测试循环，利用LLM提议规则并由脚本验证以处理非预设词汇。

Result: 1. 在400多次模拟和实时运行中，该协议在测试条件下未产生任何未检测到的误译，且覆盖率超过被动基线（d = 0.62）。
2. 注入的“袋鼠陷阱”（错误参照物附着）在100%的运行中击败了朴素指示法和纯统计学习。
3. 协议拦截了所有诱饵，并在本体论上无法区分证据时声明奎因式等价类而非猜测。
4. 面对信息员噪声表现优雅降级：每词2%噪声下无袋鼠残留，10%噪声下主要选择 abstain（弃权）而非犯错。
5. 对于超出脚本假设空间的词汇，覆盖率随LLM提议者能力提升而增加（0% -> 18% -> 72% -> 100%），且全程保持零未检测误译。

Conclusion: 正确性是协议本身的属性，而覆盖率则是仪器的属性。该研究证明了通过结构化协议可以有效解决奎因翻译不确定性问题，实现高可靠性的跨认知体系交流。

Abstract: Astrolinguistics -- communication with minds that categorize reality differently from ours -- has been purely speculative since Freudenthal's Lincos (1960). We make it experimental. Two language models with deliberately incompatible constructed lexicons (one encoding shape, color, and motion; the other fusing color with motion, encoding parity, and lacking shape) serve as informants with complete ground truth, while a fully scripted orchestrator translates between the two category systems. The central failure mode is the kangaroo effect: the silent attachment of a word to the wrong referent -- Quine's indeterminacy of translation, operationalized. Across 400+ simulated and live runs, a protocol combining cross-situational elimination, pre-registered predictive probes, active scene selection, a stricter recovery round, and quarantine produced no undetected mistranslations under the tested conditions and exceeded a passive baseline's coverage (d = 0.62). Injected kangaroo traps defeated naive ostension and pure statistical learning in 100% of runs, while the full protocol intercepted every decoy and, where discriminating evidence is ontologically unavailable, declared Quinean equivalence classes instead of guessing. Under informant noise it degrades gracefully: zero kangaroos persist up to 2% per-word noise; at 10% the protocol predominantly abstains rather than errs. Finally, words outside the scripted hypothesis space (a history-dependent relational term and an XOR contextual homonym) are recovered by a generate-and-test loop in which an LLM proposes rules and the script verifies them: coverage scales with proposer capability (0% -> 18% -> 72% -> 100%) while undetected mistranslations stayed at zero throughout. In the tested conditions, correctness is a property of the protocol; coverage is a property of the instruments.

</details>


### [154] [Comment-level Topic Drift Analysis in the Reddit Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19133)
*Steven Morse,Daniel Runfola,Trenton W. Ford*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于嵌入的动态主题建模方法，用于在大规模语料库（127亿条Reddit评论）中检测和量化评论级别的语义漂移。研究发现政治和社会争议性话题存在显著的定向漂移，而音乐和体育等领域则相对稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决如何在海量文本数据中有效检测和分析随时间演变的语义漂移及话语演变问题，特别是针对短文本在预训练语言模型生成的嵌入空间中的动态变化。

Method: 利用预训练语言模型为短文本生成上下文语义嵌入；对2006至2022年的127亿条Reddit评论进行无监督分析以识别动态演化的主题簇；提出一种在嵌入空间中分析语义漂移的方法论；修改现有方法以支持大规模分析；构建零模型比较测试以过滤虚假动态。

Result: 成功实现了大规模语料库中的语义漂移分析；发现政治和社会争议性话题的嵌入空间方向性漂移显著，主题间距离随时间系统性变化且超出零模型解释范围；相比之下，音乐和体育等领域的主题表现较为稳定。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种可扩展的方法来量化和分析大规模在线讨论中的语义漂移，揭示了不同领域话题演化模式的差异，并为理解数字话语的时间演变提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We present a novel application of embedding-based dynamic topic modeling techniques to detect and quantify topic drift at the comment level in a massive corpus. By leveraging pretrained language models to generate contextualized semantic embeddings for short text, we analyzed 12.7 billion Reddit comments spanning 2006 to 2022. Using unsupervised methods on these embeddings, we identify dynamically evolving topic clusters over time. Our primary contribution is a methodology for analysis of semantic drift and discourse evolution in the embedding space itself. We also demonstrate modifications to existing methods that enable this analysis at scale, and we propose and demonstrate a null model comparison test to filter spurious dynamics. Key findings suggest that politically and socially contentious topics exhibit significant directional drift in embedding space, with inter-topic distances changing systematically over time beyond what the null model can explain, whereas domains such as music and sports remain comparatively stable.

</details>


### [155] [ChildSafeAds Shared Task 2026: Commercial Content in Child-Facing YouTube Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19165)
*Thales Bertaglia,Catalina Goanta,Gerasimos Spanakis,Gunes Acar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ChildSafeAds是一个针对YouTube视频中可能触达儿童和青少年的商业内容共享任务。该任务包含3,360个视频，要求系统识别推广类型、产品类别及法律风险标志。数据基于SponsorBlock标记的片段，并设有四级证据访问层级以评估数据收集成本。研究发现45.5%的视频未正确使用平台广告披露机制。标签由GPT-5.4和GPT-5.6-luna生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决YouTube视频中面向儿童和青少年的商业内容识别问题，特别是那些未被正确披露的广告行为。通过构建结构化数据集和共享任务，促进对这类内容的自动化检测、分类及合规性风险评估的研究。

Method: 1. 数据构建：从939个频道选取3,360个视频，利用SponsorBlock扩展程序标记赞助商片段，并结合转录文本、视频元数据及描述页链接构建实例。
2. 任务定义：设定三个子任务（ST1：识别推广性质；ST2：分配产品类别；ST3：识别法律风险标志）。
3. 分级评估：设置四级累积证据访问级别（从转录本到链接页面），用于比较不同系统在不同数据获取成本下的表现。
4. 标签生成：经专家团队迭代优化后，使用GPT-5.4进行标注，并由GPT-5.6-luna独立标注开发集。

Result: 1. 数据集特征：45.5%的视频未能正确使用“包含付费推广”这一平台内广告披露标签，显示合规性问题普遍存在。
2. 资源提供：提供了包含详细元数据和分级证据访问权限的数据集及任务基准。
3. 模型基线：展示了GPT系列模型在自动标注任务中的能力，为后续参与系统的性能对比奠定基础。

Conclusion: ChildSafeAds共享任务提供了一个标准化的框架和数据集，用于评估系统在识别潜在未成年受众商业内容及法律风险方面的能力。通过引入数据收集成本的考量，该任务不仅关注准确性，还强调了实际应用中的效率与可行性。未来版本将纳入参赛系统的结果，进一步完善评估体系。

Abstract: ChildSafeAds is a shared task on commercial content in YouTube videos likely to reach children and teenagers. It contains 3,360 videos from 939 channels. Each instance begins with a segment submitted to SponsorBlock, an open-source crowdsourced browser extension whose users mark sponsor segments so that others can skip them. We pair the segment with its available transcript, video and channel information, and a sales or service page linked from the video description. Systems determine what kind of offer is being promoted (ST1), assign product categories (ST2), and identify legal risk flags (ST3). The evidence is divided into four cumulative access levels, from the transcript to the linked page, so results can be compared against the cost of collecting the data. 45.5\% of videos in our data failed to properly use the in-platform ad disclosure method (the ``Includes paid promotion'' label). GPT-5.4 produced the labels after the expert organiser team reviewed samples and iterated on the taxonomy, prompts and model choices. GPT-5.6-luna independently labelled the development set. This report describes the task, data and evaluation. An updated version will add participating systems and shared-task results.

</details>


### [156] [SPADE: Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19197)
*Bo Liu,Simon Yu,Yiding Jiang,Ao Qu,Andrew Zhao,Zichen Liu,Junsu Kim,Zijian Zhou,Seungone Kim,Tongzheng Ren,Mickel Liu,Hanfei Yu,Zhaorun Chen,Weiyan Shi,Paul Pu Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPADE提出了一种基于自博弈的强化学习框架，让大型语言模型同时扮演环境设计者和推理智能体。通过动态生成可执行的训练环境并优化 regret 信号，解决了传统固定环境池导致的分布停滞问题，显著提升了模型在数学、代码及多步工具使用任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言智能体的训练环境池（如人工精选或静态合成）在模型扩展时保持目标分布固定，限制了持续自我改进的能力。需要一种能产生多样化、适应性目标的动态环境生成机制。

Method: 引入SPADE框架，其中单个LLM承担双重角色：1) 环境设计者：基于预训练语料和累积记忆，编写具有OpenAI Gym风格接口的长视野可执行环境；2) 推理智能体：在这些环境中学习行动。通过计算有无特权提示时的奖励差距来估计智能体的regret，以此指导环境设计者生成处于智能体能力边缘但可行的挑战性问题。

Result: 在30B参数模型规模下，SPADE在8个数学、科学、代码和推理基准上平均比最强固定环境基线提升5.3分；在多步工具使用设置中，BFCL-v4提升5.7分，ACEBench-Agent提升13.9分。游戏场景下的优势随模型规模扩大而增长。

Conclusion: 通过将环境设计本身作为一个可学习的组件，SPADE实现了动态适应的训练环境生成，为迈向开放式自我改进提供了具体路径。

Abstract: Continuous self-improvement requires an ever-expanding pool of self-generated, diverse, adaptive goals. For language agents, existing training environment pools (hand-curated, statically synthesized, or frozen-verifier) keep the goal distribution fixed as the learner scales. We introduce SPADE (Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments), a self-play RL framework in which a single LLM plays two roles: an Environment Designer that writes complete, long-horizon training environments as executable code with an OpenAI Gym-style reset()/step() interface, and a Reasoning Agent that learns to act in them. Each is a stateful, multi-turn environment (state transitions, reward functions, and verification code), so one interface spans reasoning problems and multi-step agentic tool use. The Reasoning Agent's regret is estimated using the gap between its reward with and without privileged hints; in optimizing this regret signal the Environment Designer learns to target environments at the edge of the agent's capabilities while keeping them feasible. Through extensive experimentation, we find several components critical to success: grounding the Environment Designer on documents sampled from a large pretraining corpus, and giving it an accumulated environment memory. Scaling to 30B-parameter models, SPADE improves over the strongest fixed-environment baseline by +5.3 on average across eight held-out math, science, code, and reasoning benchmarks, and lifts the tool-use setting by +5.7 on BFCL-v4 multi-turn and +13.9 on ACEBench-Agent; on the games setting, the margin over the strongest baseline grows with model scale. By making environment design itself a learnable component, SPADE takes a concrete step toward open-ended self-improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Global Crises and National Policies: A Large Scale Analysis of Political Content in German Language Online Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18268)
*Yara Döring,Felix Bießmann*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究利用自动化政治文本分析方法，对2019-2022年间德语在线媒体（新闻和推特）进行细粒度政治偏见分析。发现国际危机（如疫情、乌克兰战争）能同步各国媒体报道，而国内议题则反映政策差异；报纸内容更稳定，推特反应更具短期波动性。该工具旨在提高媒体透明度，帮助打破算法回声室效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于算法推荐可能导致政治偏见的媒体消费模式，研究旨在通过自动化提取文本中的政治议程，揭示和分析在线媒体的政治偏见，从而促进政治 unbiased 的媒体消费并赋能公民。

Method: 采用现代政治文本分析方法，对2019-2022年间数百万篇德语文章和推文进行分析，涵盖新冠疫情和乌克兰战争初期等重大事件。比较德国与瑞士的国家报道、报纸与推特平台的 discourse 差异。

Result: 1. 国家层面：受国际事件驱动的报道具有高度主题相似性；受国内因素影响的类别则显示出因政策和制度不同而产生的分歧。2. 平台层面：在疫情期间，报纸和推特围绕核心议题趋同，但报纸政治内容更稳定，推特呈现短期事件驱动的峰值波动。3. 总体结论：国际危机是强大的同步力量，暂时超越了国家和媒体形式的差异。

Conclusion: 自动化政治分析使在线媒体中的政治议程透明化，赋能公民；同时帮助媒体机构弥合算法驱动的“回声室”与知情、平衡的公共话语之间的差距。

Abstract: Today most media content is consumed based on algorithmic recommendations. Evidence suggests that this can lead to politically biased media consumption patterns. Automated extraction of political agendas from texts can reveal and analyze political biases in online media -- and thus help fostering politically unbiased media consumption. Here we employ modern political text analysis methods demonstrating the potential of automated fine-grained political bias analysis in online media. We conduct an analysis of political content in German language online media during the period 2019--2022, encompassing several million articles and tweets covering events with profound societal impact globally and nationally, the COVID-19 pandemic and the beginning of the war in Ukraine. Our analysis identifies thematic similarity between national (German and Swiss) reporting, particularly for categories driven by international events. We also find divergences emerging in domestically influenced categories, reflecting differences in national policies and institutional structures. A comparison of newspaper and Twitter discourse reveals that both media converge around a shared core during the pandemic, yet differ in intensity and temporal dynamics. Newspapers exhibit more stable political content, while Twitter reacts through short-lived event-driven spikes. These findings indicate that international crises act as a powerful synchronizing force on political content in classical media, temporarily overriding both national and media-form differences. Our automated political analysis empowers citizens by rendering political agendas in online media transparent. This transparency also enables media outlets to bridge the gap between algorithm-driven echo chambers and a more informed, balanced public discourse.

</details>


### [158] [Multimedia Asset Personalization via Multimodal Embeddings at Netflix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18322)
*Emma Yanyang Kong,Aditya Deshpande,Bowei Yan,Asad Abbasi,Santiago Castro,Avneesh Saluja,David Fagnan,Ashish Rastogi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Netflix利用多模态嵌入（CLIP和自研MediaFM）重构推荐系统，解决冷启动问题，提升跨画布及视频预览的个性化效果，并通过离线代理任务加速模型迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于ID交互历史的资产选择模型无法感知内容本身，导致对新上线标题/资产的冷启动服务失效，且单一模态在视频预览个性化上表现不足。

Method: 1. 引入预训练图像嵌入（CLIP）增强双塔模型，实现跨画布知识迁移；2. 开发三模态基础模型MediaFM，融合视觉、音频和文本信号用于视频预览；3. 构建与线上结果相关的离线代理任务以筛选模型。

Result: 单模型替代五个独立模型并显著改善冷启动性能；MediaFM在离线和在线A/B测试中均优于纯视觉基线；离线代理任务有效加速了实验和产品化周期。

Conclusion: 多模态嵌入能有效重塑推荐系统生产流程，结合工程优化（如共享基础设施和低延迟服务）可实现高效部署，为从业者提供可借鉴的经验。

Abstract: Personalized promotional assets, namely artwork images and video preview clips, are critical to content discovery on Netflix. Traditional models for asset selection rely on ID-based interaction history, leaving them blind to asset content and unable to serve newly launched titles and assets. We describe how multimodal embeddings reshaped production systems at Netflix and report transferable lessons for practitioners adopting foundation-model embeddings into recommender systems. First, pretrained image embeddings unlock cross-title, cross-canvas knowledge transfer. Augmenting a two-tower model with CLIP image embeddings lets a single model serve all five Netflix artwork canvas types, replacing five separately trained per-canvas models and substantially improving cold-start performance. A lightweight extension reuses CLIP's joint text-image space to make artwork personalization query-aware in search. Second, multimodality decisively beats any single modality for video preview personalization. We describe MediaFM, our in-house tri-modal foundation model trained on a large-scale corpus of shots from the Netflix show catalog, fusing visual (SeqCLIP), audio (wav2vec 2.0), and timed-text signals; adopted for video preview personalization, it outperforms strong visual-only baselines both offline and in online A/B tests. Third, a simple offline proxy task whose performance correlates with online outcomes can accelerate the experimentation and productization cycle. Predicting the popularity-based winner from embeddings alone ranks embedding models and versions, pruning the choice space before any end-to-end integration or A/B test; it now gates every new MediaFM checkpoint. We also share the production engineering decisions (shared embedding infrastructure, low-latency serving, cheap screening) that made these deployments viable, along with the design tradeoffs and failure modes we encountered.

</details>


### [159] [AI in Search Reduces Publisher Referrals Without Improving User Experience: Experimental Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18352)
*Stephanie T. Wang,Jeffrey Gleason,Yakov Bart,Christo Wilson,Danaé Metaxa*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 一项针对Google Search的预注册实地实验表明，生成式AI功能（AI Overviews和AI Mode）显著降低了用户向内容发布者的点击率，损害了用户体验与信任，并对依赖流量生存的在线出版商造成负面经济影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨将生成式AI整合入网络搜索后，如何改变用户导航和信息评估方式，以及由此对提供底层内容的出版商产生的下游影响。

Method: 在Google Search平台进行了一项预注册实地实验（N=1,100），通过对比移除AI Overviews和AI Mode、仅保留AI Mode等不同情境，估算其对用户行为、感知及出版商流量的因果效应。

Result: 移除AI Overviews和AI Mode会增加向出版商的点击率；而仅使用AI Mode体验会降低点击率，并侵蚀用户对Google上信息的使用体验和信任度。

Conclusion: 生成式AI在网络搜索中的整合重塑了在线注意力分配，给维持搜索平台和整体信息生态系统的在线出版商带来了经济后果。

Abstract: The integration of generative AI into web search delivers synthesized answers to user queries, changing how people navigate and assess information, while raising concerns about the downstream impacts on publishers who supply the underlying content. We conduct a preregistered field experiment (N=1,100) on Google Search, the dominant online search platform, to estimate the causal effects of AI Overviews and AI Mode on user behavior, perceptions, and publisher traffic. We show that removing AI Overviews and AI Mode increases click-through rates to publishers, while an AI Mode-only experience reduces click-through rates and erodes user experience and trust in information found on Google. These findings show that integrating generative AI into web search reshapes online attention, with economic consequences for the online publishers that sustain both search platforms and the overall information ecosystem.

</details>


### [160] [More Context, Same Budget: Dual-Bounded Relational Recall Beyond Top-K Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18448)
*Thomson D. Nguy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出双界关系召回（DBRR）方法，在固定检索预算下，通过分配相关性种子和受限图邻域上下文，比传统扁平Top-k检索显著提高了证据恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在相同检索预算限制下，传统扁平Top-k排名无法完全恢复问题所需全部证据的问题，探索如何通过利用证据间的关系来优化上下文分配。

Method: 采用双界关系召回（DBRR），将固定的检索预算分配给基于相关性选定的种子节点和受限的图相邻上下文，并与使用相同相关性排序阶段和最大检索单元/令牌数量的匹配扁平Top-k检索进行对比测试。

Result: 在HotpotQA数据集的7,405个FullWiki问题上，Primary DBRR相比基准线使完整支持性证据恢复率提高了23.8个百分点；桥接类问题提升28.7点，比较类问题提升4.2点；真实关系优于随机邻居和控制组。

Conclusion: 在相同的上下文预算下，完整证据检索不仅取决于哪些项目排名最高，还取决于上下文如何围绕它们分配；关系型分配能恢复出扁平Top-k检索遗漏的完整证据集。

Abstract: More context does not require a larger retrieval budget. Under the same ceiling, a retrieval system can recover more of the evidence a question requires by following relationships between evidence that flat top-k ranking leaves behind. We test that proposition with Dual-Bounded Relational Recall (DBRR), which allocates a fixed retrieval budget between relevance-selected seeds and bounded graph-adjacent context, against matched flat top-k retrieval using the same relevance-ranking stage and the same maximum number of retrieval units and tokens. The outcome is complete recovery of the official HotpotQA supporting-evidence set for each question.
  Across 7,405 FullWiki questions, the Primary DBRR allocation increased complete supporting-evidence recovery by 23.8 percentage points over its matched flat baseline (paired risk difference 0.2377; question-level bootstrap 95% interval 0.2269 to 0.2489). It improved 1,952 questions, tied on 5,261, and harmed 192. Bridge questions drove the effect, with a 28.7-point increase; comparison questions showed a smaller 4.2-point difference. In a prespecified, evaluation-only diagnostic population, real relationships also outperformed random-neighbor and degree-preserving shuffled-graph controls.
  The result is straightforward: under the same context budget, complete-evidence retrieval depends not only on which items rank highest, but on how context is allocated around them. Relational allocation recovered complete evidence sets that flat top-k retrieval left incomplete.

</details>


### [161] [OneModel: A Unified Foundation for Platform-Scale Multi-Scenario Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18606)
*Yinqi Zhang,Peiyu Hu,Yuntian Tang,Siying Gu,Jiahao Liang,Longxin Kou,Haiqing Hu,Shuman Zhuang,Yubin Xu,Chenggen Sun,Bin Ye,Donghui Xu,Zhaoyu Liu,Jiang Rong,Yuting Jia,Zhaokai Luo,Leilei Ma,Yiying Xie,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了OneModel，一个用于小红书多业务流（推荐、广告、商家服务）的统一最终排序框架。通过共享事件序列和场景感知信息调制解决数据碎片化问题，并在生产环境中实现了显著的业务指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平台级推荐系统通常将有机推荐、广告和商家服务等不同业务流分开维护独立的排序系统，导致用户表征碎片化且工程成本高昂。旨在通过统一框架整合多流排序，优化用户表征并降低工程复杂度。

Method: 提出OneModel统一框架：1) 将异构行为映射为共享事件序列；2) 使用面向动作的主干网络学习长上下文用户表征；3) 引入“场景感知信息调制”以平衡跨流迁移与特定流专业化；4) 采用分层用户表征、多目标训练及在线服务优化（特征分解、预取、共享塔计算等）。

Result: 在小红书生产环境部署验证：离线性能优于强基线；在线A/B测试显示，探索Feed中停留时间+0.33%、互动+1.25%；Feed广告中广告价值+3.43%、CTR+8.18%；商家推荐中DGMV+1.1867%、GPM+2.1585%。

Conclusion: 统一的多元流排序作为生产基础架构是有效的，能够显著提升各业务线的核心指标，同时具备随上下文长度和模型容量扩展的良好特性。

Abstract: Platform-scale recommender systems often span multiple business streams such as organic recommendation, advertising, and merchant services, where user behaviors form a continuous cross-stream trajectory. Maintaining separate ranking systems fragments user representations and increases engineering cost. We propose \textbf{OneModel}, a unified framework for multi-stream final ranking. OneModel maps heterogeneous behaviors into shared event sequences, learns long-context user representations with an action-oriented backbone, and introduces \emph{Scenario-aware Information Modulation} to balance cross-stream transfer and stream-specific specialization. For production deployment, OneModel further adopts stratified user representation, multi-objective training, and optimized online serving with feature decomposition, user feature prefetching, shared user-tower computation, and graph-level inference optimization. We deploy OneModel in production at \emph{Xiaohongshu}, where it delivers consistent offline gains over strong baselines and scales favorably with context length and model capacity. Online A/B tests improve Time Spent by \textbf{+0.33\%} and Engagement by \textbf{+1.25\%} in Explore Feed, lift advertising value by \textbf{+3.43\%} and CTR by \textbf{+8.18\%} in Feed Advertising, and raise DGMV by \textbf{+1.1867\%} and GPM by \textbf{+2.1585\%} in Merchant Recommendation, validating unified multi-stream ranking as an effective production foundation.

</details>


### [162] [PILOT Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18637)
*Jiuning Lin,Ruiquan Lan,Xiaodong Zhu,Bin Zhang,Chengyu Lai,Chuxin Chen,Dimin Wang,Hongtao Cheng,Jialin Zhu,Lingqing Zhang,Shuai Zhong,Tao Wang,Weipeng Huang,Yinjiang Cai,Yinnan Song,Yuan Liu,Zhibo Xiao,Zhixin Ma,Zihong Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了PILOT，一种基于LLM的代理框架，用于主动优化推荐系统。通过实验管理器、搜索规划器和记忆策展人三个角色的协作，实现了从被动响应到主动设计控制实验的转变。在淘宝平台的实验中，PILOT相比基线方法ROAM显著提升了各项核心指标（如IPV和交易金额），并将搜索效率从53.3%提升至93.3%，全程无需人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推荐系统优化代理方法本质上是反应式的，仅根据观察到的指标变化调整参数。它们缺乏主动设计受控实验、在用户细分层面个性化策略以及在任务间积累可重用实验方法论的能力。

Method: 提出PILOT（Proactive Insight Learner for Online Tree-Experiments）框架，在一个受限的控制循环中组织三个角色：(1) 实验管理器（Experiment Manager）：驱动完整的实验生命周期（任务接收、观测治理、异常恢复、事后分析），仅从规则生成的合法命令包中选择操作；(2) 搜索规划器（Search Planner）：仅在管理器请求时提议用于用户细分个性化的候选决策树；(3) 记忆策展人（Memory Curator）：异步将实验结果提炼为策略级领域知识和具有溯源的方法论，且与主循环故障隔离。确定性服务强制执行所有安全、统计和权限边界。

Result: 在淘宝平台部署，使用5个实验桶进行对比。相比基线ROAM（无生命周期治理的自由探索代理），PILOT实现了更高的提升：IPV最高+1.40%（ROAM为+1.00%），核心IPV +1.60%（ROAM为+0.90%），交易笔数 +0.96%（ROAM为+0.60%），交易金额 +1.50%（ROAM为+1.13%）。此外，搜索效率从53.3%大幅提升至93.3%（提升40个百分点），且整个实验周期无需人工干预。

Conclusion: PILOT通过结构化的角色分工和严格的约束机制，成功实现了推荐系统优化的主动性、细粒度个性化和经验复用，显著优于传统的反应式代理方法，证明了其在大规模工业场景中的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Existing agentic approaches for recommendation system optimization remain fundamentally reactive: they adjust parameters in response to observed metric changes but lack the ability to proactively design controlled experiments, personalize strategies at the user-segment level, or accumulate reusable experimental methodology across tasks. We present PILOT (Proactive Insight Learner for Online Tree-Experiments), an LLM-agent framework that organizes three roles within a constrained control loop where deterministic services enforce all safety, statistical, and permission boundaries: (1) an Experiment Manager that drives the full experiment lifecycle -- task intake, observation governance, anomaly recovery, and postmortem -- by selecting only from a rule-generated legal-command envelope; (2) a Search Planner that proposes candidate decision trees for user-segment-level personalization, invoked only when the Manager requests planning; and (3) a Memory Curator that asynchronously distills experiment outcomes into strategy-level domain knowledge and provenance-tracked methodology, failure-isolated from the main loop. The Manager makes the agent proactive, the Planner enables population-level personalization beyond global tuning, and the Curator turns every completed task into a learning opportunity for the next. Deployed on Taobao's platform with 5 experimental buckets, PILOT is compared against ROAM(Reactive Optimization with Agent-driven Moves), a free-exploration agent without lifecycle governance or structured hypothesis testing. PILOT achieves up to +1.40% IPV, +1.60% Core IPV, +0.96% transaction count, and +1.50% transaction amount, improving over ROAM's best results (+1.00% IPV, +0.90% Core IPV, +0.60% transaction count, +1.13% transaction amount) while raising search efficiency from 53.3% to 93.3% (+40 pp), with no human intervention throughout the experimental cycle.

</details>


### [163] [GreekBarRetrieval: A Benchmark for Greek Statutory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18752)
*Ernest Beta,Odysseas S. Chlapanis,Dimitrios Galanis,Ion Androutsopoulos*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出希腊法律检索基准GreekBarRetrieval，发现LLM查询重构能显著提升BM25和密集检索性能，其中基于ReAct循环的BM25取得最佳排序指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决希腊语法律依据的法律问答中检索环节被忽视的问题，弥补现有希腊语基准（GreekBarBench）缺乏检索任务的不足。

Method: 构建包含283道考题、案情事实及6,308个候选法条的基准；测试三种BM25变体和九种密集检索器；引入十轮类ReAct LLM查询重构循环以优化检索效果。

Result: 原始密集检索在Recall@100上优于稀疏检索；LLM查询重构帮助BM25缩小差距并提升密集检索效果；经ReAct重构后，BM25在Recall@100、nDCG和MAP指标上均优于所有其他测试检索器及伪相关反馈等方法。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的查询重构是提升法律条文检索性能的关键技术，特别是结合迭代式重构策略可显著优化稀疏检索器的表现。

Abstract: Statutory retrieval is necessary for citation-grounded legal question answering, but remains underexplored for Greek. We introduce GreekBarRetrieval, a public retrieval benchmark derived from, and complementing GreekBarBench, which did not include retrieval. The new benchmark comprises 283 bar-exam questions, each accompanied by the facts of the case it refers to, and 6,308 candidate statutory articles to retrieve from. Questions and facts are stated in everyday language, but need to be mapped to the formal terminology of statutes and their abstract legal concepts. A further complication is that not all of the case facts are relevant to each question of a case. Experimenting with three BM25 variants and nine dense retrievers, we find that vanilla dense retrieval far outperforms vanilla sparse retrieval in Recall@100. However, LLM-based query reformulation helps BM25 close that gap, while also improving dense retrieval. With a ten-round ReAct-like LLM reformulation loop that we introduce, BM25 improves further in Recall@100 and obtains the best nDCG and MAP scores of all tested retrievers. Query reformulation also outperforms pseudo-relevance feedback, sparse-dense fusion, and English translation.

</details>


### [164] [GateDiffInt: Gate-Mediated Controllable Diffusion and Multi-Intent LLM Distillation for User Behavior Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18764)
*Jialong Duan,Zichen Zhang,Zirui Tu,Zheng Zhang,Zepeng Li,Qingyao Cui,Qinwen Wang,Yudan Liu,Luo Yang,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出GateDiffInt框架，通过可控前向扩散过程与双门控机制解决行为序列中的噪声-意图耦合问题，并利用大语言模型蒸馏结构化意图，最终在工业级排序场景中实现点击率预测性能的显著提升及GMV增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排序模型仅隐式编码用户意图，难以解耦不同强度和时序尺度的结构化意图。行为序列中存在“噪声-意图耦合”（NIC）现象：噪声稀释真实意图，而缺乏结构化意图先验又使去噪缺乏明确目标，导致模型性能受限。

Method: 1. 构建GateDiffInt意图交互框架；2. 利用最终转化信号联合对齐序列去噪与意图提取；3. 应用带双门控的可控前向扩散过程增强和去噪行为序列；4. 使用大语言模型作为教师，将长期、短期、潜在和转化四种结构化意图蒸馏至轻量级学生模型；5. 通过注意力机制深度融合增强后的序列表示与结构化意图表示，生成意图感知表示用于转化率预测。

Result: 在公开数据集和大规模工业数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法相比强基线模型具有持续的性能提升。在线A/B测试覆盖数亿日活跃用户，GateDiffInt带来了显著的GMV（商品交易总额）增长，并已部署于主要流量入口。

Conclusion: GateDiffInt有效解决了噪声-意图耦合问题，证明了其在工业级推荐排序系统中的有效性和生产就绪性，为理解复杂用户行为序列提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Existing ranking models encode intent only implicitly, making it hard to disentangle structured intents of varying strength and temporal scale. Noise and intent in behavior sequences are mutually reinforcing---we call this Noise--Intent Coupling (NIC). Noise dilutes true intents, while the lack of structured intent priors leaves denoising without a clear target.To address NIC, we propose GateDiffInt, an intent interaction framework for industrial ranking. It uses the final conversion signal to jointly align sequence denoising and intent extraction. GateDiffInt applies a controllable forward diffusion process with dual gating to enhance and denoise behavior sequences. A large language model then acts as teacher to distill four structured intents---long-term, short-term, latent, and conversion---into a lightweight student model. The enhanced sequence and structured intent representations are deeply fused via attention to produce intent-aware representations for conversion-rate prediction.Extensive experiments on public and large-scale industrial datasets show consistent gains over strong baselines. In online A/B tests serving hundreds of millions of daily active users, GateDiffInt delivers substantial GMV improvements and has been deployed to primary traffic, confirming both effectiveness and production readiness.

</details>


### [165] [SIDScope: A Diagnostic Resource for Semantic-ID Interfaces in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18779)
*Jiandong Ding,Huijie Qin,Tiandeng Wu,Yi Cao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SIDScope是一个用于诊断语义ID映射（Semantic-ID mappings）质量的工具，通过追踪来源、验证结构和路径结果，揭示了映射健康度的多维度特性，包括前缀对齐与检索暴露的相关性、目标路径的非唯一检索风险，以及刷新后模型复用的额外检查需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义ID映射发布时缺乏对相干性、结构暴露、生成路径解析及刷新后重新验证必要性的明确说明，导致使用者难以评估接口健康和决定何时需要重新验证或调整模型。

Method: 开发SIDScope工具，标准化物品到代码的产物，验证来源和连接，分析映射结构，比较成对的修订版本，并追踪生成轨迹中的路径-物品结果。在Amazon和Yelp数据集上的九个来源追踪的tokenizer导出上进行了实验，包括八个可执行路由和一个可审计快照。

Result: 1. 接口健康是多信号而非单一标量的；2. 前缀对齐在检索使用SID前缀时强烈跟踪保留候选暴露，但在评分独立于前缀时减弱；3. 有效的目标路径可能在未唯一检索到目标物品的情况下存在，差距为1.2-3.0个百分点；4. 修复映射本身不能恢复继承的生成器，模型复用需要单独的手递检查。

Conclusion: SIDScope提供了冻结的证据摘要、合规报告、轨迹标签、表构建器和CPU-only验证器，支持关于工件就绪性、接口风险和模型复用前重新验证的决策。

Abstract: Semantic-ID mappings are reusable interfaces between item tokenizers and generative recommenders, yet released mappings rarely state whether they are coherent, what structure they expose, how generated paths resolve, or what must be revalidated after a refresh. SIDScope is a source-traced diagnostic resource for these decisions. It normalizes item-to-code artifacts, verifies provenance and joins, profiles mapping structure, compares paired revisions, and accounts for path-to-item outcomes in generated traces. Across nine source-traced tokenizer exports from seven families on Amazon and Yelp data - eight executable routes plus one auditable snapshot - SIDScope reveals that interface health is multi-signal rather than scalar. Its central finding is mechanism-conditional: prefix alignment strongly tracks held-out candidate exposure when retrieval consumes SID prefixes, then weakens as scoring becomes prefix-independent. Trained trace accounting exposes a second hidden gap: a valid target path can survive without uniquely retrieving the target item by 1.2-3.0 percentage points. A refresh case establishes a third: repairing the mapping does not by itself restore an inherited generator; model reuse requires a separate handoff check. The package provides frozen evidence summaries, conformance reports, trace labels, table builders, and CPU-only verifiers. It supports decisions about artifact readiness, interface risks, and revalidation before model reuse.

</details>


### [166] [Think-to-Personalize: Unifying Reasoning and Retrieval for User-Centric Personalized Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18855)
*Angqing Jiang,Gaoming Zhang,Jianchun Song,Kena Qi,Dayao Chen,Wei Lin,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Think-to-Personalize (TTP)框架，通过显式推理用户历史行为生成意图增强查询，结合强化学习优化检索效果，在电商搜索中显著提升订单量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的密集检索模型多将其视为静态编码器，忽略其推理能力；且标准模型以查询为中心，无法有效利用用户历史上下文解决稀疏/模糊查询带来的意图差距；个性化检索方法依赖隐式嵌入交互，缺乏从噪声历史行为中消除歧义的能力。

Method: 1. 构建TTP框架：对用户购买序列进行推理，显式推导潜在个性化需求并生成意图增强查询，进而编码为统一密集嵌入。2. 两阶段训练范式：(1) 监督微调（SFT）建立冷启动能力；(2) 使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）的强化学习（RL）阶段，使推理过程与检索效用对齐。

Result: 在自有和公开基准测试中显著优于最先进基线；在线A/B测试显示订单量提升+0.46%，验证了其实用有效性。

Conclusion: TTP建立了推理驱动的个性化密集检索新范式，证明了将显式意图推理与LLM结合在解决电商搜索意图差距方面的优势。

Abstract: Dense retrieval has become a cornerstone of modern local-lifestyle e-commerce search by encoding queries and items into semantic embedding spaces. While recent advancements have transitioned from BERT-based embedding models to Large Language Models (LLMs), most approaches still treat LLMs as static text encoders, neglecting their inherent reasoning capabilities. Furthermore, standard dense retrieval models remain query-centric, which is insufficient in e-commerce scenarios where sparse and ambiguous queries create an intent gap that can only be bridged by the rich context of user history. Meanwhile, existing personalized retrieval methods typically rely on implicit embedding interactions, which lack the reasoning capability to effectively disambiguate user intent from noisy historical behaviors. To address these challenges, we propose Think-to-Personalize (TTP), a novel framework that unifies explicit user-centric intent reasoning with dense retrieval. By reasoning over the user's historical purchase sequence, TTP explicitly deduces latent personalized needs and generates an intent-enhanced query, which is then encoded into a unified dense embedding. Specifically, we design a two-stage training paradigm: (1) a Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) stage that establishes cold-start capabilities; and (2) a Reinforcement Learning (RL) stage that aligns the reasoning process with retrieval utility using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Extensive experiments on both proprietary and public benchmarks demonstrate that TTP significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Furthermore, in online A/B tests, it achieved a +0.46% lift in order volume, validating its practical effectiveness and establishing a new paradigm for reasoning-driven personalized dense retrieval.

</details>


### [167] [rEDMRec: Distilling Large Language Model Reasoning into an Editable Experience Memory for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18952)
*Minh Hoang Nguyen,Tung Le,Huy Tien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出rEDMRec框架，将大语言模型（LLM）的推理过程压缩为四种可编辑的经验通道（长期偏好、短期上下文、物品感知、反事实硬负样本），由轻量级学生模型直接检索记忆进行推荐，从而避免每次请求都重新调用昂贵的教师LLM进行推理，实现了推理成本与深度的解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的推荐系统虽然能通过显式推理提升质量，但每次排序请求都需重复昂贵的推理过程，且生成的推理结果通常是一次性的，无法复用，也难以随用户口味变化进行修正或检查。

Method: 提出rEDMRec方法：1) 将教师LLM的推理蒸馏到四个类型的经验通道中；2) 使用LLM记忆控制器管理记忆的增删改查操作，并通过多智能体辩论机制优化记忆条目；3) 轻量级学生模型仅通过检索该结构化记忆来对候选项进行排序，无需再次调用教师LLM。

Result: 在ML-1M、Amazon Beauty和Steam数据集上，针对十种学生骨干网络，rEDMRec在HR@1指标上优于零样本、少样本和RAG基线，在ML-1M上较次优基线最高提升13.3%。消融实验显示短期上下文在所有容量层级下均有效，其他通道效果依赖模型容量。辩论优化降低了7.4%的记忆冗余，并在六轮优化后使HR@1最高提升0.029。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM推理压缩为可管理的结构化记忆，rEDMRec成功解耦了在线推理成本与推理深度，不仅显著提升了推荐性能，还解决了传统LLM推荐中推理不可复用和难以维护的问题。

Abstract: Large language models can improve recommendation quality by reasoning explicitly over user history and candidate items - for example, extracting a user's preferences or explaining why one item fits better than another - rather than mapping history directly to a ranked list. This reasoning, however, is expensive to repeat on every ranking request and, once produced, is typically consumed once and discarded, leaving it neither reusable across future requests nor easy to inspect or correct as user tastes drift. Our insight is that reasoning does not need to be regenerated at every call if it can instead be compressed once into a compact, structured memory that a lightweight model retrieves from. We propose rEDMRec, which distills a teacher LLM's reasoning into four typed, editable experience channels - long-term preference, short-term context, item-perception, and counterfactual hard-negative comparisons - maintained by an LLM memory controller that performs Add/Delete/Modify/Keep operations and refines entries via K-agent debate. A lightweight student LLM then ranks candidates purely by retrieving from this memory, without invoking the teacher again, decoupling online inference cost from reasoning depth. Across ML-1M, Amazon Beauty, and Steam and ten student backbones, rEDMRec improves HR@1 over zero-shot, few-shot, and RAG on every backbone, and over GraphRAG on most backbones, with Impv up to 13.3% vs. the second-best baseline on ML-1M. Channel ablations show that short-term context is the only channel that helps consistently across capacity tiers, whereas long-term, item-perception, and counterfactual contributions are capacity-dependent (and can reverse on the strongest students); debate-based memory optimization lowers bank duplication by 7.4 percentage points while raising downstream HR@1 by up to +0.029 over six optimization epochs.

</details>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [APICURON: a reactive infrastructure for credit attribution across distributed research data ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18958)
*Adel Bouhraoua,Mehdi Zoubiri,Gavin Farrell,Maria Cristina Aspromonte,Alex Bateman,Henning Hermjakob,Maria Victoria Nugnes,Daniela Raciti,Nicholas Stiffler,Geert van Geest,Ulrike Wittig,Karen Yook,Federica Quaglia,Silvio C. E. Tosatto*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: APICURON是一个用于生物信息学数据策展贡献的信用归属平台，通过实时记录策展活动并关联ORCID，解决传统学术评估中专家策展工作不被认可的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决数据驱动生物学研究中，由专家策展人生成的结构化知识在现有学术评估体系中缺乏正式认可和激励的问题。

Method: 开发APICURON平台，采用实时捕捉策展事件而非延迟批量报告的方式，将策展行为转化为可验证的工作单元；允许独立资源自定义识别模型，并通过ORCID连接研究人员身份与持续学术档案。

Result: APICURON已成功整合到多个生物知识库和数据资源中，适用于多样化工作流程，并能支持对非传统研究产物（如培训材料和研究软件）的贡献认可。

Conclusion: 该平台有效弥合了生物数据策展工作与学术评价之间的鸿沟，提供了一种灵活、可验证且历史可追溯的贡献认定基础设施。

Abstract: Data-driven biology relies on structured knowledge generated by expert biocurators, yet this work remains largely unrecognized in traditional academic assessments. To bridge this gap, we present the updated APICURON platform, a credit-attribution infrastructure that formally acknowledges these scientific contributions. Rather than relying on delayed batch reporting, the system captures curation events as they happen and transforms them into verifiable units of work. This design allows independent resources to define and update their own recognition models while preserving the historical record of each contribution. For researchers, APICURON highlights recent activity alongside lifetime achievements and connects verified activities to persistent academic profiles via ORCID. APICURON has been successfully integrated across biological knowledgebases and data resources, demonstrating its application to diverse workflows. Extending beyond biodata resources, it also supports recognition of non-traditional research artefacts, including training materials and research software, without imposing a rigid definition of contribution.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Position: Collusion Risks Among AI Reasoning Agents Justify Certification Requirements for Making Market Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18078)
*Matthew Riemer,Tommaso Tosato,Amin Memarian,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Glen Berseth,Irina Rish,Guillaume Dumas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出具备思维链推理能力的AI代理易产生共谋行为，建议通过行为认证来防止其在市场中破坏竞争。实验显示即使人类提示不共谋，DeepSeek-R1代理仍倾向于隐性共谋，且这种共谋无法通过语义检测识别，导致无证据的共谋结果。因此，在部署前需进行基于代表性情境的行为认证以确保市场稳定与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将具备思维链推理能力的AI代理整合到社会中，可能会模糊独立企业间“竞争”与“共谋”的法律证据区分，尽管经济损害的本质未变。现有的法律框架难以应对这种由AI引发的、无明确合谋意图但实质造成共谋后果的情况，亟需新的监管或认证机制。

Method: 1. 理论分析：探讨AI代理思维链推理能力对市场竞争结构的影响及法律证据界限的挑战。
2. 实验验证：在伯特兰寡头定价（Bertrand oligopoly pricing）领域使用DeepSeek-R1代理进行实验。
3. 干预测试：测试人类提示（prompting）对代理共谋行为的影响。
4. 可检测性分析：评估另一个LLM能否通过语义分析检测到思维链中的共谋倾向。
5. 引导策略探索：尝试将代理的思维链引导至极端共谋或高度竞争状态，并验证其泛化能力。

Result: 1. DeepSeek-R1代理表现出持续的隐性共谋倾向，即使在被提示不要共谋的情况下也是如此。
2. 代理的思维链可以被引导至极度共谋或高度竞争的状态，但这种变化在语义上具有隐蔽性，无法被其他LLM通过分析推理轨迹检测到。
3. 部署推理型代理会导致无共谋证据或意图显示的共谋经济结果。
4. 提供了初步证据表明，可以通过通用方式将代理引导至有效竞争均衡状态。

Conclusion: 由于AI代理可能导致无证据的共谋行为，必须在将其部署到现实市场之前开发并实施全面的“行为认证”机制。该认证应基于代理在代表性情境下的观察行为，而非仅依赖意图或语义分析，以确保市场的稳定性、效率和合法性。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI agents with chain-of-thought reasoning capabilities are predisposed to exhibit collusive behavior and should be required to obtain behavioral certification before making decisions that affect economic markets. This is because integrating these agents into society could collapse the legal evidentiary distinction between competition and collusion among independent firms without eroding the economic harm distinction. Experiments with DeepSeek-R1 agents in the Bertrand oligopoly pricing domain reveal a tendency towards tacit collusion that persists even when humans prompt the agents not to collude. We further show that the chain-of-thought of these agents can be steered toward either extremely collusive or highly competitive behavior in a way that is not semantically detectable by another LLM analyzing the reasoning traces. As a result, deploying reasoning agents for market decisions leads to collusive economic outcomes without any evidence of conspiracy or intent. Thus, certification based on observed behavior in representative situations is necessary to prevent collusion. We provide preliminary evidence that such agents can be steered in a generalizable way toward efficient competitive equilibria. However, developing a comprehensive behavioral certification will be required before these models can be deployed in real-world markets while ensuring their stability and efficiency.

</details>


### [170] [Position: Profiling Game Worlds by Transition Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18079)
*Lele Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出过渡复杂度轮廓（TCP）作为评估游戏世界建模和强化学习环境难度的标准化指标集，旨在解决现有研究缺乏对转换预测问题难度量化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的游戏世界建模（GWM）和强化学习（RL）研究通常混淆了环境本身的难度与算法性能，因为论文很少量化在特定接口（如像素/令牌/潜在变量）处底层转换预测问题的实际难度。

Method: 提出过渡复杂度轮廓（TCP），这是一套小型、可复现的指标集合，通过三个维度表征环境的诱导转换核：(i) 内在的一步分支性；(ii) 当可观测时的交互引起的不确定性和对手影响；(iii) 通过标准化探测曲线衡量的时间/空间依赖跨度。该报告包含明确的参考分布、协议随机性和版本化的测量预算，以确保基准测试间的可比性。

Result: 详细描述了常见游戏家族和现代“神经游戏引擎”领域如何填充这一复杂度景观，并证明了TCP能够提供跨基准的可比数值。

Conclusion: 呼吁将TCP作为GWM和RL论文中的标准基准元数据及必需统计量，以规范领域内的环境难度评估。

Abstract: Game world modeling (GWM) and reinforcement learning (RL) are often confounded because research papers rarely quantify how difficult the underlying transition prediction problem is at the declared interface (pixels/tokens/latents with finite history). We propose the Transition Complexity Profile (TCP): a small, reproducible set of metrics that characterizes an environment's (or gameplay dataset's) induced transition kernel by (i) intrinsic one-step branching, (ii) interaction-induced uncertainty and opponent influence when observable, and (iii) temporal/spatial dependency span via standardized probe curves. TCP is reported with an explicit reference distribution, protocol stochasticity, and a versioned measurement budget (sampling/resampling and fixed probe compute), enabling comparable numbers across benchmarks. We outline how common game families and modern "neural game engine" domains populate this landscape and call for TCP to become standard benchmark metadata and a required statistic in GWM and RL papers.

</details>


### [171] [Self-prompting and cross-model consensus enable reproducible data extraction from scientific literature with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19025)
*Valentin Romanov,Monique Bax,Steven Niederer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究前沿基于浏览器的LLM在从科研文章中提取语境化数据方面的性能，发现其虽能高效执行数据提取和提示词生成，但在文献自主发现和科学语境理解上仍有局限，最终提出一种“专家制定标准、模型交叉验证、人类解决争议”的人机协作审计工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确从研究文章中提取细微且具语境化的数据是一项耗时且劳动密集型的工作，旨在探索前沿大语言模型（LLMs）在此任务中的实际效能与局限性。

Method: 评估了四种递进式工作流程：1) 使用专家精心设计的提示词进行数据提取；2) 让LLM根据简单指令自动生成提示词；3) 尝试LLM自主发现研究文献；4) 利用LLM从已发布的指南中创建新数据集。

Result: 1) LLM在给定专家提示词时表现良好，但难以解读科学语境和细微差别；2) LLM自生成的提示词效果几乎等同于专家撰写；3) LLM自主发现文献困难，存在遗漏或幻觉问题；4) LLM创建的数据集接近人类专家判断，但仍需人工介入。

Conclusion: 定义了一种可审计的分工模式：专家设定证据标准，模型交叉检查重复提取结果，研究人员解决争议案例，从而在不放弃专家监督的前提下实现科学数据整理的规模化扩展。

Abstract: Accurately extracting nuanced, contextualized data from research articles is laborious and time intensive. Here, we investigate the performance of frontier, browser-based large language models (LLMs) to extract highly contextualized information. We demonstrate four escalating workflows, 1) given an expert curated prompt and research articles, most frontier LLMs perform well at data extraction, however can struggle with interpreting scientific context and nuance, 2) given simple instructions, LLMs can author their own prompts which were almost as eNective as expert-written prompts, 3) autonomous discovery of research literature was diNicult, agents either missed or hallucinated references, and 4) LLMs can create new datasets from published guidelines that closely match human-expert judges, but still require a human-in-the-loop. Together, these findings define an auditable division of labour in which experts specify the evidence standard, models cross-check repeated extractions and researchers resolve disputed cases, providing a practical route to scaling scientific data curation without relinquishing expert oversight.

</details>


### [172] [Large Language Models in Mental Health: A Systematic Review of Applications, Innovations, and Ethical Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18080)
*Yisong Chen,Yifan Gao,Sijing Yu,Chuqing Zhao,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大型语言模型（LLMs）在健康领域的应用，涵盖社交媒体分析、临床对话代理、治疗支持工具等。通过整合多学科研究数据，实现抑郁早期检测、自杀风险评估及个性化治疗支持。文章强调了提示工程和多模态融合技术的重要性，并讨论了相关的伦理与监管挑战，倡导建立安全、公平的责任框架以推动LLMs在心理健康护理中的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在全面梳理和评估大型语言模型在健康领域特别是心理健康方面的应用现状，解决如何有效利用LLM进行疾病检测、治疗支持以及应对由此产生的伦理和社会技术挑战的问题。

Method: 对涉及社交媒体帖子、电子病历和多模态输入等多样化数据源的跨学科研究进行文献回顾与分析；重点考察LLM模型进展、标注策略、提示工程技巧及多模态融合技术在心理健康诊断中的应用。

Result: 1. 确认了LLM在抑郁早期检测、自杀风险评估、个性化治疗支持和心理教育内容生成方面的有效性。
2. 指出了先进的LLM架构和优化标注策略能显著提升模型的可解释性和临床相关性。
3. 强调了提示工程在领域适应中的关键作用。
4. 展示了结合文本、语音和传感器数据的多模态融合技术能改善心理健康的诊断和监测效果。

Conclusion: 尽管LLMs在心理健康领域展现出巨大潜力，但仍面临显著的伦理、社会技术和监管障碍。建议建立相应的框架，以确保这些模型在现实世界的心理健康护理中能够被安全、公平且负责任地部署和应用。

Abstract: We present a review on the applications of large language models (LLMs) in health, e.g., social media analysis, clinical conversational agents, therapy support tools, prompt engineering, multimodal learning, and ethical considerations. We integrate findings from interdisciplinary studies utilizing diverse data sources such as social media posts, electronic medical records, and multimodal inputs to enable early detection of depression, suicide risk assessment, personalized therapy support, and psychoeducational content generation. Our review highlights advancements in LLM models and annotation strategies that enhance interpretability and clinical relevance, while we also emphasize the critical role of prompt engineering for domain adaptation. We also discuss emerging multimodal fusion techniques integrating text, speech, and sensor data for improved mental health diagnosis and monitoring. Finally, we address ongoing ethical, sociotechnical, and regulatory challenges, and advocate frameworks to ensure safe, equitable, and accountable deployment of LLMs in real-world mental health care.

</details>


### [173] [Position: Behavioral Systems Require Behavioral Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18081)
*Manuel Cherep,Nikhil Singh,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 文章主张AI智能体评估应从单纯关注性能结果转向关注行为过程，借鉴行为科学提出通过系统观察、干扰和解释来评估AI行为的科研路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估方法主要关注绩效结果，忽视了产生这些结果的底层行为过程；随着AI智能体在动态环境中表现出类似行为系统的特征，亟需建立相应的评估体系。

Method: 借鉴行为科学的理论与方法，提出一套严格的“行为测试”研究议程，包括从动作序列中恢复决策策略、构建隔离行为差异的环境以及探测多智能体系统中的涌现动态。

Result: 提出了一套针对AI智能体的系统性行为评估框架，旨在通过观察、干扰和解释其行动来深入理解其行为机制。

Conclusion: 该研究方向为发展“AI行为科学”提供了路线图，强调将AI智能体视为行为系统进行严谨的科学评估。

Abstract: Artificial agentic systems increasingly operate as behavioral systems by interacting with dynamic environments, pursuing goals, and adapting over time. Yet, current evaluation methods largely focus on performance outcomes, not the underlying behavioral processes that produce them. This paper argues that AI agents must be evaluated like other behavioral systems: through systematic observation, perturbation, and interpretation of their actions. We draw on lessons from the behavioral sciences to motivate this position, and propose a research agenda focused on developing rigorous behavioral tests. These include methods for recovering decision strategies from action sequences, constructing environments that isolate behavioral differences, and probing emergent dynamics in multi-agent systems. Taken together, these directions offer a roadmap for developing a science of AI behavior.

</details>


### [174] [Position: Current Model Cards Are Insufficient for Downstream Governance of Open-Weight Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18086)
*Sungwon Chae,Keonwoo Kim,Hoki Kim,Jaeyeon Ju,Sangchul Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张针对开放权重基础模型（OWFMs）的有效治理，需将模型卡片、使用政策（AUPs）和许可证整合为多层级框架，以弥补现有监管在安全性上的缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管模型卡片广泛用于透明度披露，但现有框架未能充分告知下游开发者和用户OWFMs带来的独特安全挑战；同时，标准开源许可证可能削弱使用政策的执行力。

Method: 分析了Hugging Face上托管的500个模型卡片，识别由现有监管方法（如模型传承、对齐溯源及实证行为观察）留下的安全缺口，并论证了标准开源许可证对OWFMs的不适应性。

Result: 发现现有模型卡片缺乏关键的安全信息，且标准开源许可证不适合OWFMs，可能导致使用政策执行力的减弱。

Conclusion: 应将模型卡片、AUPs和许可证演变为集成的安全工件，构建一个综合性的治理框架，协调统一信息、规范和法律三个维度。

Abstract: The growth of open-weight foundation models (OWFMs) has prompted the AI community to re-evaluate strategies for effective downstream governance. Although model cards have been widely adopted as transparency artifacts in model repositories, existing frameworks often fail to adequately inform downstream developers and users about the distinct safety challenges posed by OWFMs. This position paper analyzes 500 model cards hosted on Hugging Face and argues that effective governance of OWFMs requires a multi-layered approach integrating three complementary components: (i) model cards, (ii) acceptable use policies (AUPs), and (iii) licenses. To motivate this claim, we identify a safety gap left by existing regulatory approaches, including model heritage, alignment provenance, and empirically observed behaviors, through an analysis of model cards with safety-critical information. We further argue that standard open-source licenses (OSLs) are not well suited for OWFMs and may weaken the enforceability of AUPs. Building on these observations, we outline directions for evolving model cards, AUPs, and licenses into integrated safety artifacts to enable a more comprehensive governance framework that coherently integrates informational, normative, and legal dimensions.

</details>


### [175] [A Metamorphic Artificial Age Score Decision-Support Prototype for Flight-Log-Based Drone Propeller Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18088)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于多指标融合的多态人工年龄评分（AAS）决策支持原型，利用DronePropA数据集的飞行日志监测无人机螺旋桨健康，实现故障分级与维护优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机螺旋桨故障的影响分散在多个飞行日志通道中，而非表现为单一诊断信号，导致传统单指标监测难以全面评估安全风险与可靠性。

Method: 从2024年DronePropA公开数据集中选取历史真实飞行日志，计算六项健康指标（轨迹跟踪误差、姿态不稳定性、推力命令负担、电机命令不平衡、ESC命令不稳定性、电池水平压力），通过归一化基准线、候选评分策略、变异充分性关系及冗余调整的AAS公式进行综合评估。

Result: 在相同速度和轨迹下对一例健康基线和三种缺陷螺旋桨案例进行回顾性评估：健康案例为常规监控；严重程度1（主要受ESC命令不稳定性影响）进入维护审查；严重程度2（最大电机和ESC命令负担）和严重程度3（最大轨迹跟踪误差）触发强制检查。结果表明故障效应可通过不同操作通道显现。

Conclusion: 多指标决策支持层对于飞行后维护优先级排序和自主系统监管至关重要，能有效应对分布式故障信号带来的监测挑战。

Abstract: Drone propeller faults can create safety and reliability risks when their effects are distributed across multiple flight-log channels rather than appearing as a single diagnostic signal. This paper proposes a Metamorphic Artificial Age Score (AAS) decision-support prototype for flight-log-based drone propeller health monitoring. Using selected historical real flight logs from the 2024 DronePropA public dataset, the framework computes six health-related indicators from raw MATLAB matrices: trajectory tracking error, attitude instability, thrust-command burden, motor-command imbalance, ESC-command instability, and battery-level stress. These indicators are normalized relative to a healthy baseline and evaluated through candidate scoring policies, metamorphic adequacy relations, and a redundancy-adjusted AAS formulation. In this context, AAS is used as a structural policy-adequacy and burden measure rather than as a chronological age measure. A controlled retrospective evaluation was performed using one healthy baseline and three defective propeller cases under the same speed profile and trajectory. The healthy case was assigned to routine monitoring. The Severity 1 case was dominated by ESC-command instability and assigned to maintenance review. The Severity 2 case reached maximum motor-command and ESC-command burden, while the Severity 3 case reached maximum trajectory tracking error; both triggered mandatory inspection. The results show that propeller fault effects may appear through different operational channels, supporting the need for a multi-indicator decision-support layer for post-flight maintenance prioritization and autonomous-system oversight.

</details>


### [176] [Position: Multi-Agent Systems Should Prioritize Concurrency Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18092)
*Xin Yang,Letian Li,Zimo Ji,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Wenyuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM多智能体系统（MAS）的可靠性下降本质上是并发控制问题，主张将显式的并发控制机制作为核心设计要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决增加智能体数量导致系统可靠性降低的问题，揭示传统归因于协调或通信失败的表象背后隐藏的并发缺陷。

Method: 通过理论分析，将MAS中的故障模式映射到经典的并发异常（如脏读、丢失更新），论证长推理窗口加剧了状态不一致风险。

Result: 确认许多MAS失败源于并发读取和写入共享状态时的竞争条件，特别是由LLM推理延迟导致的陈旧读取和不一致结果。

Conclusion: MAS框架应将并发控制视为一等公民（first-class design concern），通过冲突检测、隔离保证和结构化资源访问来确保系统可靠性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) promise scalable collaboration, yet adding agents often reduces reliability. This position paper argues that many MAS failures are fundamentally concurrency control problems: agents concurrently read and write shared state, and long LLM inference windows amplify the risk of stale reads, lost updates, and inconsistent outcomes. Failure modes commonly attributed to coordination or communication breakdowns can be mapped directly onto classical concurrency anomalies.
